Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ode variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 726 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 432 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by ode generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 726, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 432. QED.
